<div class="paragraph paragraph__removePadding--bottom">
<p style="text-align: center;"><strong><span style="font-size: x-large;">Want to become a wine buff? </span></strong></p><p style="text-align: center;">Do you know your Pinot from your Sauvignon? Would you love to learn how to sip and savour the flavour of wine? Discover the best food pairings and impress every guest with your amazing knowledge of wine. Then take the Aldiploma, designed with our resident wine expert and Mistress of Wine, Sam Caporn.</p></div>

<h1 class="category-name" style="text-align: center;"><strong><font size="5">How to make this Bonfire Night amazing!</font></strong></h1> <p style="text-align: center;">Just go to Aldi, of course. We’ve got everything you need to make the night go with a real bang, from fun sparklers to jaw-dropping fireworks, and tasty recipes to warming drinks. Remember though, follow the fireworks code and stay safe.</p></div>
